The `define`s in eval.scm's source code set up the inner environment. So:\n\n1. The primitives in the inner evaluator are Scheme closures that delegate to the outer evaluator's primitives, which ultimately delegate to the host.\n\nThis is the essence of metacircular evaluation!\n\nNow let me write the actual code. I need to be very careful about correctness.\n\n```scheme\n;; eval.scm - Metacircular Evaluator\n;; Reads a file path from stdin, then interprets that file.\n;; Remaining stdin is passed through to the interpreted program.\n\n;; ============================================================\n;; Utility predicates\n;; ============================================================\n\n(define (tagged-list? expr tag)\n  (and (pair? expr) (eq? (car expr) tag)))\n\n(define (self-evaluating? expr)\n  (or (eq? expr #t)\n      (eq? expr #f)\n      (string? expr)\n      (number? expr)))\n\n;; ============================================================\n;; Environment\n;; An environment is a pair: (parent-env . frame)\n;; A frame is an association list: ((name . value) ...)\n;; ============================================================\n\n(define (make-env parent)\n  (cons parent '()))\n\n(define (env-parent env)\n  (car env))\n\n(define (env-frame env)\n  (cdr env))\n\n(define (set-env-frame! env frame)\n  (set-cdr! env frame))\n\n(define (env-lookup env name)\n  (if (null? env)\n      (error \"Unbound variable\" name)\n      (let ((binding (assq name (env-frame env))))\n        (if binding\n            (cdr binding)\n            (env-lookup (env-parent env) name)))))\n\n(define (assq key alist)\n  (if (null? alist)\n      #f\n      (if (eq? (car (car alist)) key)\n          (car alist)\n          (assq key (cdr alist)))))\n\n(define (env-define! env name value)\n  (let ((frame (env-frame env)))\n    (let ((binding (assq name frame)))\n      (if binding\n          (set-cdr! binding value)\n          (set-env-frame! env (cons (cons name value) frame))))))\n\n(define (env-set! env name value)\n  (if (null? env)\n      (error \"Unbound variable in set!\" name)\n      (let ((binding (assq name (env-frame env))))\n        (if binding\n            (set-cdr! binding value)\n            (env-set! (env-parent env) name value)))))\n\n(define (extend-env names values parent)\n  (let ((env (make-env parent)))\n    (let loop ((ns names) (vs values))\n      (if (null? ns)\n          env\n          (begin\n            (env-define! env (car ns) (car vs))\n            (loop (cdr ns) (cdr vs)))))\n    env))\n\n;; ============================================================\n;; Error handling\n;; ============================================================\n\n(define (error msg obj)\n  (display \"Error: \")\n  (display msg)\n  (newline)\n  #f)\n\n;; ============================================================\n;; Procedure representation\n;; A compound procedure is: (procedure params body env)\n;; A primitive procedure is: (primitive name impl-fn)\n;; ============================================================\n\n(define (make-procedure params body env)\n  (list 'procedure params body env))\n\n(define (compound-procedure? obj)\n  (tagged-list? obj 'procedure))\n\n(define (procedure-params p)\n  (car (cdr p)))\n\n(define (procedure-body p)\n  (car (cdr (cdr p))))\n\n(define (procedure-env p)\n  (car (cdr (cdr (cdr p)))))\n\n(define (make-primitive impl)\n  (list 'primitive impl))\n\n(define (primitive? obj)\n  (tagged-list? obj 'primitive))\n\n(define (primitive-impl obj)\n  (car (cdr obj)))\n\n;; ============================================================\n;; eval\n;; ============================================================\n\n(define (eval expr env)\n  (cond\n    ((eq? expr #t) expr)\n    ((eq? expr #f) expr)\n    ((string? expr) expr)\n    ((number? expr) expr)\n    ((null? expr) '())\n    ((symbol? expr) (env-lookup env expr))\n    ((tagged-list? expr 'quote) (car (cdr expr)))\n    ((tagged-list? expr 'if) (eval-if expr env))\n    ((tagged-list? expr 'define) (eval-define expr env))\n    ((tagged-list? expr 'set!) (eval-set! expr env))\n    ((tagged-list? expr 'lambda) (eval-lambda expr env))\n    ((tagged-list? expr 'let) (eval-let expr env))\n    ((tagged-list? expr 'begin) (eval-sequence (cdr expr) env))\n    ((tagged-list? expr 'progn) (eval-sequence (cdr expr) env))\n    ((tagged-list? expr 'cond) (eval-cond expr env))\n    ((pair? expr) (apply-procedure (eval (car expr) env)\n                                    (eval-args (cdr expr) env)))\n    (else (error \"Cannot evaluate\" expr))))\n\n(define (eval-if expr env)\n  (let ((condition (eval (car (cdr expr)) env)))\n    (if (eq? condition #f)\n        (if (null? (cdr (cdr (cdr expr))))\n            '()\n            (eval (car (cdr (cdr (cdr expr)))) env))\n        (eval (car (cdr (cdr expr))) env))))\n\n(define (eval-define expr env)\n  (let ((pattern (car (cdr expr))))\n    (if (pair? pattern)\n        ;; Function definition shorthand\n        (env-define! env\n                     (car pattern)\n                     (make-procedure (cdr pattern)\n                                     (cdr (cdr expr))\n                                     env))\n        ;; Variable definition\n        (env-define! env\n                     pattern\n                     (eval (car (cdr (cdr expr))) env)))))\n\n(define (eval-set! expr env)\n  (env-set! env\n            (car (cdr expr))\n            (eval (car (cdr (cdr expr))) env)))\n\n(define (eval-lambda expr env)\n  (make-procedure (car (cdr expr))\n                  (cdr (cdr expr))\n                  env))\n\n(define (eval-let expr env)\n  (let ((bindings (car (cdr expr)))\n        (body (cdr (cdr expr))))\n    (let ((names '())\n          (values '()))\n      (let loop ((bs bindings))\n        (if (null? bs)\n            'done\n            (let ((b (car bs)))\n              (set! names (append names (list (car b))))\n              (set! values (append values (list (eval (car (cdr b)) env))))\n              (loop (cdr bs)))))\n      (eval-sequence body (extend-env names values env)))))\n\n(define (eval-cond expr env)\n  (let loop ((clauses (cdr expr)))\n    (if (null? clauses)\n        '()\n        (let ((clause (car clauses)))\n          (if (or (eq? (car clause) 'else)\n                  (not (eq? (eval (car clause) env) #f)))\n              (eval-sequence (cdr clause) env)\n              (loop (cdr clauses)))))))\n\n(define (eval-sequence exprs env)\n  (if (null? exprs)\n      '()\n      (if (null? (cdr exprs))\n          (eval (car exprs) env)\n          (begin\n            (eval (car exprs) env)\n            (eval-sequence (cdr exprs) env)))))\n\n(define (eval-args args env)\n  (if (null? args)\n      '()\n      (cons (eval (car args) env)\n            (eval-args (cdr args) env))))\n\n(define (apply-procedure proc args)\n  (cond\n    ((compound-procedure? proc)\n     (eval-sequence (procedure-body proc)\n                    (extend-env (procedure-params proc)\n                                args\n                                (procedure-env proc))))\n    ((primitive? proc)\n     ((primitive-impl proc) args))\n    (else (error \"Not a procedure\" proc))))\n\n;; ============================================================\n;; Primitives\n;; ============================================================\n\n(define (prim-+ args)\n  (if (null? args)\n      0\n      (+ (car args) (prim-+ (cdr args)))))\n\n(define (prim-- args)\n  (if (null? (cdr args))\n      (- 0 (car args))\n      (- (car args) (car (cdr args)))))\n\n(define (prim-* args)\n  (if (null? args)\n      1\n      (* (car args) (prim-* (cdr args)))))\n\n(define (prim-/ args)\n  (/ (car args) (car (cdr args))))\n\n(define (prim-= args)\n  (= (car args) (car (cdr args))))\n\n(define (prim-< args)\n  (< (car args) (car (cdr args))))\n\n(define (prim-> args)\n  (> (car args) (car (cdr args))))\n\n(define (prim-<= args)\n  (<= (car args) (car (cdr args))))\n\n(define (prim->= args)\n  (>= (car args) (car (cdr args))))\n\n(define (prim-cons args)\n  (cons (car args) (car (cdr args))))\n\n(define (prim-car args)\n  (car (car args)))\n\n(define (prim-cdr args)\n  (cdr (car args)))\n\n(define (prim-null? args)\n  (null? (car args)))\n\n(define (prim-pair? args)\n  (pair? (car args)))\n\n(define (prim-eq? args)\n  (eq? (car args) (car (cdr args))))\n\n(define (prim-equal? args)\n  (equal? (car args) (car (cdr args))))\n\n(define (prim-not args)\n  (not (car args)))\n\n(define (prim-and args)\n  (if (null? args)\n      #t\n      (if (car args)\n          (prim-and (cdr args))\n          #f)))\n\n(define (prim-or args)\n  (if (null? args)\n      #f\n      (if (car args)\n          #t\n          (prim-or (cdr args)))))\n\n(define (prim-display args)\n  (display (car args)))\n\n(define (prim-newline args)\n  (newline))\n\n(define (prim-getchr args)\n  (getchr))\n\n(define (prim-putchr args)\n  (putchr (car args)))\n\n(define (prim-read args)\n  (read))\n\n(define (prim-symbol? args)\n  (symbol? (car args)))\n\n(define (prim-string? args)\n  (string? (car args)))\n\n(define (prim-number? args)\n  (number? (car args)))\n\n(define (prim-set-car! args)\n  (set-car! (car args) (car (cdr args))))\n\n(define (prim-set-cdr! args)\n  (set-cdr! (car args) (car (cdr args))))\n\n(define (prim-fopen args)\n  (fopen (car args) (car (cdr args))))\n\n(define (prim-fclose args)\n  (fclose (car args)))\n\n(define (prim-fgetchr args)\n  (fgetchr (car args)))\n\n(define (prim-fputchr args)\n  (fputchr (car args) (car (cdr args))))\n\n(define (prim-fdisplay args)\n  (fdisplay (car args) (car (cdr args))))\n\n(define (prim-fread args)\n  (fread (car args)))\n\n(define (prim-symbol args)\n  (symbol (car args)))\n\n(define (prim-chr args)\n  (chr (car args)))\n\n;; ============================================================\n;; Initial environment setup\n;; ============================================================\n\n(define (make-initial-env)\n  (let ((env (make-env '())))\n    (env-define! env 'else #t)\n    (env-define! env '+ (make-primitive prim-+))\n    (env-define! env '- (make-primitive prim--))\n    (env-define! env '* (make-primitive prim-*))\n    (env-define! env '/ (make-primitive prim-/))\n    (env-define! env '= (make-primitive prim-=))\n    (env-define! env '< (make-primitive prim-<))\n    (env-define! env '> (make-primitive prim->))\n    (env-define! env '<= (make-primitive prim-<=))\n    (env-define! env '>= (make-primitive prim->=))\n    (env-define! env 'cons (make-primitive prim-cons))\n    (env-define! env 'car (make-primitive prim-car))\n    (env-define! env 'cdr (make-primitive prim-cdr))\n    (env-define! env 'null? (make-primitive prim-null?))\n    (env-define! env 'pair? (make-primitive prim-pair?))\n    (env-define! env 'eq? (make-primitive prim-eq?))\n    (env-define! env 'equal? (make-primitive prim-equal?))\n    (env-define! env 'not (make-primitive prim-not))\n    (env-define! env 'and (make-primitive prim-and))\n    (env-define! env 'or (make-primitive prim-or))\n    (env-define! env 'display (make-primitive prim-display))\n    (env-define! env 'newline (make-primitive prim-newline))\n    (env-define! env 'getchr (make-primitive prim-getchr))\n    (env-define! env 'putchr (make-primitive prim-putchr))\n    (env-define! env 'read (make-primitive prim-read))\n    (env-define! env 'symbol? (make-primitive prim-symbol?))\n    (env-define! env 'string? (make-primitive prim-string?))\n    (env-define! env 'number? (make-primitive prim-number?))\n    (env-define! env 'set-car! (make-primitive prim-set-car!))\n    (env-define! env 'set-cdr! (make-primitive prim-set-cdr!))\n    (env-define! env 'fopen (make-primitive prim-fopen))\n    (env-define! env 'fclose (make-primitive prim-fclose))\n    (env-define! env 'fgetchr (make-primitive prim-fgetchr))\n    (env-define! env 'fputchr (make-primitive prim-fputchr))\n    (env-define! env 'fdisplay (make-primitive prim-fdisplay))\n    (env-define! env 'fread (make-primitive prim-fread))\n    (env-define! env 'symbol (make-primitive prim-symbol))\n    (env-define! env 'chr (make-primitive prim-chr))\n    env))\n\n;; ============================================================\n;; Main\n;; ============================================================\n\n;; Read the file path from stdin\n(define file-path (read))\n\n;; Open and interpret the file\n(let ((f (fopen file-path \"r\")))\n  (if f\n      (let ((env (make-initial-env)))\n        (let loop ()\n          (let ((expr (fread f)))\n            (if expr\n                (begin\n                  (eval expr env)\n                  (loop))\n                (begin\n                  (fclose f)\n                  'done)))))\n      (begin\n        (display \"Error: Could not open file \")\n        (display file-path)\n        (newline))))\n```\n\nWait, I need to check some things:\n\n1. `list` function - is it available in the host? No, there's no `list` built-in. I'm using `list` in `make-procedure` and `make-primitive`. I need to define it.\n\n2. `append` function - also not available in the host. I'm using it in `eval-let`.\n\nLet me define helper functions for list operations:\n\n```scheme\n(define (list2 a b)\n  (cons a (cons b '())))\n\n(define (list3 a b c)\n  (cons a (cons b (cons c '()))))\n\n(define (list4 a b c d)\n  (cons a (cons b (cons c (cons d '())))))\n\n(define (append2 a b)\n  (if (null? a)\n      b\n      (cons (car a) (append2 (cdr a) b))))\n```\n\nActually, let me implement a proper `list` function:\n\n```scheme\n(define (list . args)\n  ;; This uses the host's variadic lambda\n  ;; But since we're defining this in eval.scm source,\n  ;; it runs in the host and has access to variadic lambdas\n  \n  ;; Actually, define with a dot would be: (define (list . args) ...)\n  ;; But that's not how define works. Let me use lambda:\n  )\n```\n\nHmm, how to define a variadic function in the host? Looking at interp.py:\n\n```python\nelif car == \"lambda\":\n    params = []\n    param_list = expr.cdr.car\n    while param_list is not None:\n        params.append(param_list.car)\n        param_list = param_list.cdr\n    ...\n```\n\nThere's no dotted-tail support in lambda! The params are just a flat list. So variadic lambdas are NOT supported by the host.\n\nBut wait, `+` and `*` are variadic primitives in the host. So I can use them. But I can't define my own variadic functions.\n\nFor `list`, I can avoid using it. Instead of `(list 'procedure params body env)`, I can write:\n```scheme\n(define (make-procedure params body env)\n  (cons 'procedure (cons params (cons body (cons env '())))))\n```\n\nThis is more verbose but avoids needing `list`.\n\nSimilarly, for `append`, I need to implement it:\n```scheme\n(define (append lst1 lst2)\n  (if (null? lst1)\n      lst2\n      (cons (car lst1) (append (cdr lst1) lst2))))\n```\n\nWait, but I use `append` in `eval-let`. Let me think of a way to avoid it.\n\nIn `eval-let`, I'm building up `names` and `values` lists:\n```scheme\n(set! names (append names (list (car b))))\n(set! values (append values (list (eval (car (cdr b)) env))))\n```\n\nInstead, I can reverse the approach - build the lists in reverse and then work with them as-is:\n\n```scheme\n(define (eval-let expr env)\n  (let ((bindings (car (cdr expr)))\n        (body (cdr (cdr expr))))\n    (let ((names '())\n          (values '()))\n      (let loop ((bs bindings))\n        (if (null? bs)\n            (eval-sequence body (extend-env (reverse names) (reverse values) env))\n            (let ((b (car bs)))\n              (set! names (cons (car b) names))\n              (set! values (cons (eval (car (cdr b)) env) values))\n              (loop (cdr bs))))))))\n```\n\nHmm, but `reverse` is not built-in either. Let me define it:\n```scheme\n(define (reverse lst)\n  (let loop ((lst lst) (acc '()))\n    (if (null? lst)\n        acc\n        (loop (cdr lst) (cons (car lst) acc)))))\n```\n\nActually wait, I should also check: the host supports variadic lambdas in some way? Let me not worry about it for now and instead ensure my evaluator can handle the test programs correctly.\n\nLet me now write the complete eval.scm:\n\n```scheme\n;; eval.scm - Metacircular Evaluator\n;; Reads a file path from stdin, opens and interprets that file.\n;; Remaining stdin is forwarded to the interpreted program's (read) calls.\n;; Output from the interpreted program goes to stdout via (display) and (newline).\n\n;; ============================================================\n;; Helpers (run in host, used to build the evaluator)\n;; ============================================================\n\n(define (reverse lst)\n  (define (rev-iter lst acc)\n    (if (null? lst)\n        acc\n        (rev-iter (cdr lst) (cons (car lst) acc))))\n  (rev-iter lst '()))\n\n;; ============================================================\n;; Procedure and environment representation\n;; A compound procedure is a list: (procedure params body env)\n;; A primitive is a list: (primitive impl)\n;; An environment is a pair: (parent . frame)\n;; A frame is an alist: ((name . value) ...)\n;; ============================================================\n\n(define (tagged-list? expr tag)\n  (and (pair? expr) (eq? (car expr) tag)))\n\n(define (make-procedure params body env)\n  (cons 'procedure (cons params (cons body (cons env '())))))\n\n(define (compound-procedure? obj)\n  (tagged-list? obj 'procedure))\n\n(define (procedure-params p) (car (cdr p)))\n(define (procedure-body p) (car (cdr (cdr p))))\n(define (procedure-env p) (car (cdr (cdr (cdr p)))))\n\n(define (make-primitive impl)\n  (cons 'primitive (cons impl '())))\n\n(define (primitive? obj)\n  (tagged-list? obj 'primitive))\n\n(define (primitive-impl obj) (car (cdr obj)))\n\n;; ============================================================\n;; Environment operations\n;; ============================================================\n\n(define (make-env parent)\n  (cons parent '()))\n\n(define (env-parent env) (car env))\n(define (env-frame env) (cdr env))\n\n(define (set-env-frame! env frame)\n  (set-cdr! env frame))\n\n(define (env-lookup env name)\n  (if (null? env)\n      (error \"Unbound variable\" name)\n      (let ((b (assq name (env-frame env))))\n        (if b\n            (cdr b)\n            (env-lookup (env-parent env) name)))))\n\n(define (assq key alist)\n  (if (null? alist)\n      #f\n      (if (eq? (car (car alist)) key)\n          (car alist)\n          (assq key (cdr alist)))))\n\n(define (env-define! env name value)\n  (let ((frame (env-frame env)))\n    (let ((b (assq name frame)))\n      (if b\n          (set-cdr! b value)\n          (set-env-frame! env (cons (cons name value) frame))))))\n\n(define (env-set! env name value)\n  (if (null? env)\n      (error \"Unbound variable in set!\" name)\n      (let ((b (assq name (env-frame env))))\n        (if b\n            (set-cdr! b value)\n            (env-set! (env-parent env) name value)))))\n\n(define (extend-env names values parent)\n  (let ((env (make-env parent)))\n    (let loop ((ns names) (vs values))\n      (if (null? ns)\n          env\n          (begin\n            (env-define! env (car ns) (car vs))\n            (loop (cdr ns) (cdr vs)))))))\n\n(define (error msg obj)\n  (display \"Error: \")\n  (display msg)\n  (newline)\n  #f)\n\n;; ============================================================\n;; eval\n;; ============================================================\n\n(define (eval expr env)\n  (cond\n    ;; Self-evaluating\n    ((eq? expr #t) expr)\n    ((eq? expr #f) expr)\n    ((string? expr) expr)\n    ((number? expr) expr)\n    ((null? expr) '())\n    ;; Variable reference\n    ((symbol? expr) (env-lookup env expr))\n    ;; Special forms\n    ((tagged-list? expr 'quote) (car (cdr expr)))\n    ((tagged-list? expr 'if) (eval-if expr env))\n    ((tagged-list? expr 'define) (eval-define expr env))\n    ((tagged-list? expr 'set!) (eval-set! expr env))\n    ((tagged-list? expr 'lambda) (eval-lambda expr env))\n    ((tagged-list? expr 'let) (eval-let expr env))\n    ((tagged-list? expr 'begin) (eval-sequence (cdr expr) env))\n    ((tagged-list? expr 'progn) (eval-sequence (cdr expr) env))\n    ((tagged-list? expr 'cond) (eval-cond expr env))\n    ;; Application\n    ((pair? expr)\n     (apply-procedure (eval (car expr) env)\n                      (eval-args (cdr expr) env)))\n    (else (error \"Cannot evaluate\" expr))))\n\n(define (eval-if expr env)\n  (if (eq? (eval (car (cdr expr)) env) #f)\n      (if (null? (cdr (cdr (cdr expr))))\n          '()\n          (eval (car (cdr (cdr (cdr expr)))) env))\n      (eval (car (cdr (cdr expr))) env)))\n\n(define (eval-define expr env)\n  (let ((pattern (car (cdr expr))))\n    (if (pair? pattern)\n        ;; (define (f args) body) shorthand\n        (env-define! env\n                     (car pattern)\n                     (make-procedure (cdr pattern)\n                                     (cdr (cdr expr))\n                                     env))\n        ;; (define name value)\n        (env-define! env\n                     pattern\n                     (eval (car (cdr (cdr expr))) env)))))\n\n(define (eval-set! expr env)\n  (env-set! env\n            (car (cdr expr))\n            (eval (car (cdr (cdr expr))) env)))\n\n(define (eval-lambda expr env)\n  (make-procedure (car (cdr expr))\n                  (cdr (cdr expr))\n                  env))\n\n(define (eval-let expr env)\n  (define (collect-bindings bs names values)\n    (if (null? bs)\n        (eval-sequence (cdr (cdr expr))\n                       (extend-env (reverse names)\n                                   (reverse values)\n                                   env))\n        (let ((b (car bs)))\n          (collect-bindings (cdr bs)\n                            (cons (car b) names)\n                            (cons (eval (car (cdr b)) env) values)))))\n  (collect-bindings (car (cdr expr)) '() '()))\n\n(define (eval-cond expr env)\n  (define (loop clauses)\n    (if (null? clauses)\n        '()\n        (let ((clause (car clauses)))\n          (if (or (eq? (car clause) 'else)\n                  (not (eq? (eval (car clause) env) #f)))\n              (eval-sequence (cdr clause) env)\n              (loop (cdr clauses))))))\n  (loop (cdr expr)))\n\n(define (eval-sequence exprs env)\n  (if (null? exprs)\n      '()\n      (if (null? (cdr exprs))\n          (eval (car exprs) env)\n          (begin\n            (eval (car exprs) env)\n            (eval-sequence (cdr exprs) env)))))\n\n(define (eval-args args env)\n  (if (null? args)\n      '()\n      (cons (eval (car args) env)\n            (eval-args (cdr args) env))))\n\n(define (apply-procedure proc args)\n  (cond\n    ((compound-procedure? proc)\n     (eval-sequence (procedure-body proc)\n                    (extend-env (procedure-params proc)\n                                args\n                                (procedure-env proc))))\n    ((primitive? proc)\n     ((primitive-impl proc) args))\n    (else (error \"Not a procedure\" proc))))\n\n;; ============================================================\n;; Primitives - each takes a list of already-evaluated arguments\n;; ============================================================\n\n(define (prim-+ args)\n  (if (null? args) 0\n      (+ (car args) (prim-+ (cdr args)))))\n\n(define (prim-- args)\n  (if (null? (cdr args))\n      (- 0 (car args))\n      (- (car args) (car (cdr args)))))\n\n(define (prim-* args)\n  (if (null? args) 1\n      (* (car args) (prim-* (cdr args)))))\n\n(define (prim-/ args)\n  (/ (car args) (car (cdr args))))\n\n(define (prim-= args)\n  (= (car args) (car (cdr args))))\n\n(define (prim-< args)\n  (< (car args) (car (cdr args))))\n\n(define (prim-> args)\n  (> (car args) (car (cdr args))))\n\n(define (prim-<= args)\n  (<= (car args) (car (cdr args))))\n\n(define (prim->= args)\n  (>= (car args) (car (cdr args))))\n\n(define (prim-cons args)\n  (cons (car args) (car (cdr args))))\n\n(define (prim-car args)\n  (car (car args)))\n\n(define (prim-cdr args)\n  (cdr (car args)))\n\n(define (prim-null? args)\n  (null? (car args)))\n\n(define (prim-pair? args)\n  (pair? (car args)))\n\n(define (prim-eq? args)\n  (eq? (car args) (car (cdr args))))\n\n(define (prim-equal? args)\n  (equal? (car args) (car (cdr args))))\n\n(define (prim-not args)\n  (not (car args)))\n\n(define (prim-and args)\n  (if (null? args) #t\n      (if (car args) (prim-and (cdr args)) #f)))\n\n(define (prim-or args)\n  (if (null? args) #f\n      (if (car args) #t (prim-or (cdr args)))))\n\n(define (prim-display args)\n  (display (car args)))\n\n(define (prim-newline args)\n  (newline))\n\n(define (prim-getchr args)\n  (getchr))\n\n(define (prim-putchr args)\n  (putchr (car args)))\n\n(define (prim-read args)\n  (read))\n\n(define (prim-symbol? args)\n  (symbol?
